Tool result: task

Done. Report:

**Final content of `times.py`**
```python
import re

def parse_time(text):
    """Parse ASCII HH:MM (00:00-24:00 inclusive) into minutes from midnight."""
    if not isinstance(text, str):
        raise ValueError(f"not an HH:MM string: {text!r}")
    if re.fullmatch(r"([0-9]{2}):([0-9]{2})", text) is None:
        raise ValueError(f"not an HH:MM string: {text!r}")
    hours = int(text[:2])
    minutes = int(text[3:5])
    if minutes > 59:
        raise ValueError(f"minutes out of range: {text!r}")
    if hours > 24 or (hours == 24 and minutes != 0):
        raise ValueError(f"hours out of range: {text!r}")
    return hours * 60 + minutes
```
Interface: `parse_time(text: str) -> int` (minutes from midnight), raises `ValueError` on any non-strict match. Import-safe via `from times import parse_time` (verified).

**Probe results (all PASS)**
- Valid returns: `"00:00"→0`, `"00:01"→1`, `"08:05"→485`, `"12:00"→720`, `"23:59"→1439`, `"24:00"→1440` (each returned `int`).
- Invalid inputs all raised `ValueError`: `24:01`, `24:59`, `25:00`, `08:60`, `23:61`, `8:05`, `08:5`, `8:5`, `""`, `" 08:05"`, `"08:05 "`, `"08:05:00"`, `"08,05"`, full-width `"０８:０５"`, `08:05\n`, `\n08:05`, `8:05:00`, `-8:05`, `08:-5`, `a8:05`, `8a:05`, `08:5a`, `08:a5`, `00:00:00`, `100:00`, `:05`, `08:`, `08:5 `, `08: 05`, `None`, `b"08:05"`, `805`, `8.05`, `["08:05"]`, `("08:05",)`, `True`.
- Final aggregates: `ALL VALID PASS`, `ALL INVALID RAISED`. Module import + `from planner import free_minutes` succeeded.

**Assumptions**: `[0-9]` used (not `\w`/`\d`) to force ASCII; minutes are the mm value with no cross-field 24:00 reduction (sign handled by regex); lines matching exactly `HH:MM` (5 chars) are required — decimal sign, unicode digit, separator variants, list/tuple/None/bytes all rejected by the `isinstance(str)` + regex checks before any arithmetic. No files other than `times.py` were edited; no commits made.

def merge_windows(windows):
    """Merge (start, end) minute intervals into sorted disjoint (start, end) tuples.

    Intervals that overlap or merely touch are merged; zero-length intervals are
    dropped; input is never mutated; invalid input raises ValueError.
    """
    try:
        items = iter(windows)
    except TypeError:
        raise ValueError(
            "windows must be an iterable of (start, end) pairs, got %r" % (windows,)
        )

    intervals = []
    for pair in items:
        try:
            start, end = pair
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValueError(
                "each window must be exactly a (start, end) pair, got %r" % (pair,)
            )
        if type(start) is not int or type(end) is not int:
            raise ValueError(
                "window endpoints must be ints, got %r" % (pair,)
            )
        if start < 0 or end > 1440 or start > end:
            raise ValueError(
                "invalid window %r: need 0 <= start <= end <= 1440" % (pair,)
            )
        if start == end:
            continue
        intervals.append((start, end))

    intervals.sort()
    merged = []
    for start, end in intervals:
        if merged and start <= merged[-1][1]:
            if end > merged[-1][1]:
                merged[-1][1] = end
        else:
            merged.append([start, end])

    return [tuple(interval) for interval in merged]
---TIMES---
import re

def parse_time(text):
    """Parse ASCII HH:MM (00:00-24:00 inclusive) into minutes from midnight."""
    if not isinstance(text, str):
        raise ValueError(f"not an HH:MM string: {text!r}")
    if re.fullmatch(r"([0-9]{2}):([0-9]{2})", text) is None:
        raise ValueError(f"not an HH:MM string: {text!r}")
    hours = int(text[:2])
    minutes = int(text[3:5])
    if minutes > 59:
        raise ValueError(f"minutes out of range: {text!r}")
    if hours > 24 or (hours == 24 and minutes != 0):
        raise ValueError(f"hours out of range: {text!r}")
    return hours * 60 + minutes

6), (8, 10)]\n        )\n\n    def test_does_not_mutate_input(self):\n        original = [[0, 5], [4, 8]]\n        copied = [list(pair) for pair in original]\n        windows.merge_windows(original)\n planner.free_minutes([\"09:00-10:00\"]),\n            {\"busy\": [(540, 600)], \"free\": 1380},\n        )\n\n    def test_overlap_and_touching_merge_with_free(self):\n        self.assertEqual(\n
